SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">解决问题的执念，一旦被投射到瞬息万变的社会经济领域，便催生了我们对追求宏观调控圣杯的无尽追寻。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><h3 style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><b><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">2 镁光灯下的宏观经济</span></span></font></b></h3><h3 style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></h3><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">自然科学研究，总有大把能让人稍作休整的空档时期。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">人类</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">发现或者未发现一条自然定律，都不影响太阳每天照常升起</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">，宇宙始终如常运转</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">这</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">也</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">意味着，自然科学中的一个理论一旦被证伪，学术共同体有充分的时间去消化</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">，</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">并最终达成一致观念。通常，这过程长达几个世纪也不鲜见。例如</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">日心说在哥白尼死后于</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">1543年正式提出，1687年随牛顿《数学原理》的问世才逐步被主流接纳。到18世纪末日心说成为欧洲知识界共识，距离其首次提出已经过去250年了。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">但是，一个社会不管其宏观经济研究</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">、宏观政策的执行</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">水平如何，每天需要作出的决策一样都不会少</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">。宏观研究不等人，专家随时会被推向前台，手指下就是个威力巨大的政策按钮。于是，他大概率会结合本国特色，循着自己熟悉的学术体系，在第一时间给出权威解释，并执行政策</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">好在，对错之间的政策执行成本往往被重重噪声掩盖，很难当场辨别孰高孰低。更不必说，何为对错呢？很难有一个普遍意义上都适用的宏观理论。宏观经济的研究对象是社会经济。社会由人组成，社会制度由人制定。每个社会都有其绵延不断的市场</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">——包括劳动力市场、货币市场和商品市场。这些市场在长期博弈中形成了各具特色的制度，也就适用于不同理论范式。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">只要社会组织结构没散，总可以继续边试边错，边向前走。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><h3 style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><b><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">3简单归因的急迫性与锤头人</span></span></font></b></h3><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">宏观经济极具诱惑力的一点是，它用极为详尽的数量化方法，尽可能详尽地描绘出事实的影子</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">，从而让人误以为依靠影子就可以推断事实在未来的发展情况，甚至想改变历史发展的轨迹。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">比如</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft 0px;text-align: left;" data-pm-slice="3 3 []"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, style="font-size: 16px;">临到危机前夜，人们总哀叹飘落的雪花没有一片是无辜的，然后默认唯一的出路就是必须先救人于水火之中。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">然而，每一次危机前夜，也是高唱战歌的人们越聚越多、声势越来越浩大的时候。这时，已经有一些理智的少数派与主流分道扬镳。正是这些人，通常在经济危机中作为稳定器和出资方，帮助经济体实现灾后重生。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;" data-pm-slice="3 3 []"><font face="京華老宋体"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, []&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;,&quot;data-layout-id&quot;:null}]"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;">诚然，宏观研究正朝着更加精细的微观基础方面推进——从过往的千人一面，到按职业、年龄、收入和消费</span></span><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;">倾向细分人群，这些进展无疑大大提升了政策工具的有效性。</span></span></font><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;" data-pm-slice="1 1 [&quot;para&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;p&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;style&quot;:&quot;margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;,&quot;data-layout-id&quot;:null},&quot;node&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;font&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;face&quot;:&quot;京華老宋体&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;}]"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;">但这与本节所述困境仍有方法论上的本质区别。宏观经济理论追求的是统计加总和总量调控，而真实世界的异质性关注的是具体个体的生存抉择。</span></span></font><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);font-style: normal;">统计方法对分类变量定义的再细，那一类群体的内部仍然必定存在不可逾越的鸿沟。专业投资人和普通大众内都有不计后果的赌徒和洞察周期冷暖恪守能力圈的智者。这个差异在经济上行期很难被人发现，但在危机时期却被无限放大。</span></span></font></p><h3 style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></h3><h3 style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">5.&nbsp;</span></span><b><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">宏观经济可以做什么</span></span></font></b></h3><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">宏观经济的研究已经获得了大量短期内大体正确的基本推断。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">例如：</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><ul style="list-style-type: circle;" class="list-paddingleft-1"><li><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">放水</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">会刺激经济，但也极易推高通胀或引发资产泡沫，而且有可能因货币乘数不够无法达到预期，雪上加霜；</span></span><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;" data-pm-slice="1 1 [&quot;para&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;p&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;style&quot;:&quot;margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;,&quot;data-layout-id&quot;:null},&quot;node&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;font&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;face&quot;:&quot;京華老宋体&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;}]"><br  /></span></font></font></p></li><li><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;" data-pm-slice="1 1 [&quot;para&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;p&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;style&quot;:&quot;margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;,&quot;data-layout-id&quot;:null},&quot;node&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;font&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;face&quot;:&quot;京華老宋体&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;}]"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">加息</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">能有效抑制通胀，但也伴随着流动性枯竭、债务链条断裂和资产价格重估；</span></span></font></font></p></li><li><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">财政手段</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">会刺激经济，但也会有挤出效应；</span></span></font></p></li><li><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">减税</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">能刺激微观活力，但也会带来财政赤字与长期通胀预期。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p></li></ul><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">经济学家的一个口头禅是“一方面，但是另一方面”。所以曾有笑谈，罗斯福说，</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-style: italic;">“...give me an economist who says&nbsp;</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;font-style: italic;">'on the one hand'</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-style: italic;">, not one who always adds&nbsp;</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;font-style: italic;">'on the other hand'</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-style: italic;">.”</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(0, 82, 255);font-weight: bold;">传统凯恩斯模型</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(0, 82, 255);">研究总需求、总消费、总投资，假定失业、通胀、收入与消费之间的统计关系大体稳定，再按这些关系决定宏观政策刺激或紧缩多少。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">但在1970 年代美国石油冲击后，新古典宏观经济学派的卢卡斯批评指出，用历史数据估算出的总量关系并不稳固。宏观政策如果缺乏微观基础</span></span><span leaf="" data-pm-slice="1 1 [&quot;para&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;p&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;style&quot;:&quot;margin-top: 0px; margin-bottom: 0px;&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;,&quot;data-layout-id&quot;:null}]"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">只停留在总量关系</span></span><span leaf=""><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">，那么家庭与企业的行为预期一旦调整，政策就极有可能失效。</span></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(0, 82, 255);font-weight: bold;">新凯恩斯模型</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(0, 82, 255);">从家庭消费、企业定价出发，为宏观经济政策赋予了</span></span><b data-path-to-node="3" data-index-in-node="49" data-pm-slice="0 0 []"><span leaf=""><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(0, 82, 255);font-weight: normal;">微观经济学基础。其核心是补充了两大基石：</span></span><b data-path-to-node="4" data-index-in-node="52" data-pm-slice="0 0 []"><span leaf=""><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(0, 82, 255);font-weight: normal;">一是名义刚性</span></span></b><span leaf=""><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(0, 82, 255);font-weight: normal;">（即微观主体提供的产品与服务价格在短期内受制于菜单成本或信息摩擦而无法完全自由调整）；</span></span><b data-path-to-node="4" data-index-in-node="102"><span leaf=""><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(0, 82, 255);font-weight: normal;">二是前瞻预期</span></span></b><span leaf=""><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(0, 82, 255);font-weight: normal;">（即微观主体会根据对未来微观市场和宏观经济的预判，在当下主动调整行为）。从而揭示了政策传导的微观摩擦机制。</span></span></b></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><b data-path-to-node="3" data-index-in-node="49" data-pm-slice="0 0 []"></b></p><section style="text-align: left;margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;"><span leaf="" style="" data-pm-slice="1 1 [&quot;para&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;section&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;data-layout-id&quot;:null}]"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(0, 82, 255);">2026年诺奖得主霍尔进一步指出消费预期的变动本身不可预测，伍德福德通过对粘性价格与前瞻预期模型的宏观推演，证明了央行不能仅靠盲目的总量刺激，必须通过信誉机制与预期管理去主动锚定市场。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">这些进展打破了传统宏观总量关系的黑箱，为央行执行稳定政策提供了更具实践意义的工作路径。</span></span></section><section style="text-align: left;margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;"><span leaf="" style="" data-pm-slice="1 1 [&quot;para&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;section&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;&quot;,&quot;data-layout-id&quot;:null}]"><br  /></span></section><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">笔者</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">认为</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-style: normal;">现代宏观经济学已大概能够识别并阻断银行挤兑与流动性恐慌，运用应急工具把大萧条级别的产出断崖扼杀在萌芽状态，并用前瞻预期管理锚定市场在危机突发时的心理崩溃。</span></span><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">例如08年金融危机本可能成为1929年大萧条级别的灾难；但实际上危机造成的影响范围和时间长度都远小于预期。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">不过，满足于</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">把宏观经济</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">解释当作</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">根本</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">原因，会因错过更深层次的文化与观念的因素，南辕北辙。</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">经济学家不该把</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">数量化的结果，认定为就是更科学的本质，甚至反过来耻笑其他学科，譬如嘲笑历史、社会学的论述无法形成假设检验命题。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-style: italic;">（注：</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-style: italic;">CFA、MBA 发端于美国，他们的经济学家可以无比自信、自夸，但是他们的经济学家不会成为主流的技术官僚左右整个国家。有巴菲特这样的企业家无视经济学家的意见，马斯克也无视，当然政客川普更加无视。这也就在圣杯和井底这个矛盾之间找到了平衡——既获得了央行调节货币、财政调节流动性需求综合机制，又不至于让这门无法精准的学科过度地干扰市场。 所以他们的经济学家即使不自省所掌握之圣杯实乃井底圣杯，也无大碍。 但我们的经济学家（各行业的专家也同理）都会以更直接的方式介入资源运转。学科的特性和局限，就不得不在个体内就实现平衡。）</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><h2 style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;" data-pm-slice="2 3 []"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">三、</span></span><b><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">发展经济学与被追赶</span></span></font></b></h2><h2 style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></h2><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">谈宏观经济的剧痛，我们需要先深入了解宏观研究的土壤——发展经济学。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><h3 style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><b><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">1 从刘易斯拐点到被追赶的经济体</span></span></font></b></h3><h3 style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></h3><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">刘易斯针</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">对牙买加这类热带殖民地，</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">给出了发展经济学中的经典二元经济模型。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(0, 82, 255);">在农村劳动力转为城市劳动力的工业化初始阶段，</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(0, 82, 255);">传统部门有边际产出接近为</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(0, 82, 255);">0的剩余劳动力，</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(0, 82, 255);">劳动力无限供给。农村收入只够基本生存，城市劳动力维持在略高于农业生存收入水平。在经过刘易斯拐点后，农业总产出下降，粮食变少、粮价抬升。城市用工也必须给出更高工资才能继续吸纳农村劳动力。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">刘易斯</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">拐点模型的原文指出，这套模型主要适用于人口稠密的小农经济，且传统部门内部有大量闲置劳动力（隐性失业）。并不适用于解释西欧北欧的近代工业化。也不适用于非洲、拉美部分欠发达地区，那里的传统部门欠缺男性劳动力。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">总的来说，</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">日韩、中国台湾和改革开放后的中国大陆，与原始适用条件具备一定相似性。但欧美主要国家的适用度则并不高。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">例如</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">英国</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">因土地集中和圈地运动，农村部门不存在大量多余但仍留在土地的农民。类似的农业商品化和土地私有情况也包括如</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">荷兰</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">、</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">比利时</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">等低地国家和</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">西德</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">莱茵、鲁尔地区和</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">意大利北部</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">。在这些地区，农业劳动力的减少会立刻导致粮食</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">/经济作物的产量下降，随之而来的就是城市居民工资立刻上涨。另外，如东德普鲁士地区和</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">法国</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">，虽有大量农业人口，前者多属于庄园农奴/半农奴，后者持有碎片化小块土地。两个地区的农民要么不能，要么不愿意离开农村，都不符合刘易斯原始模型中假设的边际产出接近0的闲置劳动力。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><br  /></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">唯一相近的，可能是意大利南部存在大量农村隐性失业。至于</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">美国</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">，虽有大量乡村人口（主要是黑人佃农）向城市工业转移，但美国作为移</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">民经济体，移民潮也不断补充劳动力，令</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">“拐点”模糊。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><section style="text-align: left;margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;" nodeleaf=""><a href="/?view=art&scope=all&pic=353-0" class="article-gallery-link" aria-label="在图库中查看大图"><img &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">后来的</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(0, 82, 255);">广义刘易斯拐点中不再约束农村劳动力的</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, 16px;color: rgb(0, 82, 255);">“边际产出为0”，只要求：农业劳动力持续转移直至稀缺，农业工资开始上涨，带动全社会非熟练劳动力工资持续上行。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(0, 82, 255);">辜朝明的被追赶理论进一步把拐点泛化为整个经济体廉价劳动力供给由无限转为稀缺，全社会非熟练劳动力议价能力上升。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">在此简化中，劳动力来源既可以是农村农民、国内隐性失业人口，也包含海外移民。并在拐点后新增了两段，一是工资</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">- 利润正向循环的</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-weight: bold;">黄金时代</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">；另外一个是工资抬升后产业外迁、本土低技能劳工承压的</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);font-weight: bold;">被追赶阶段</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">日</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">本是适配</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">刘易斯拐点</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">程度很高</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">的案例，也是经济学界经典的东亚样本。战后日本农村存在大量隐性剩余农业劳动力，1950 年代中后期农村劳动力逐步枯竭，农业工资快速上涨，制造业普工薪资持续上行，收入倍增计划随之开启。韩国以及中国台湾适配度仅次于日本，大约在 1970 年代中后期跨过拐点。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">待到劳动力供给转向短缺，经济体便进入</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">黄金时代</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">，辜朝明统计了不同经济体在这一阶段的跨度：美国自</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">1945 到 1970 年代中期约四十年，日本自六十年代初至 1990 年约三十年，韩国与中国台湾则是 1980 至 2005 年约二十年。辜朝明、李录判断中国大约在 2010 年前后迈入这一阶段</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">黄金时代之后便是</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">被追赶阶段</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">。美国自七十年代中期开始，本土钢铁、汽车产业受到日本、西德制造业冲击，产业持续向东亚转移，本土蓝领薪资长期停滞。九十年代广场协定后的日本，本土汽车电子产业向外迁移，本土制造业投资收缩，低技能劳动者收入停滞，资本海外收益抬升。</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">2005 年后的韩国与中国台湾，大量代工产业先转移至中国大陆，后续继续流向越南、印尼，本土制造业面临空心化压力。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">然而，要清醒看到，</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">一战前繁荣的欧洲、改革开放前的中国以及多数非洲地区</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">，都无法被纳入这一节提到的统一叙事。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">无论是</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">“大衰退”，还是“被追赶的经济体”，仅仅是对</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">某个国家</span></span></font><font &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;" data-pm-slice="1 1 [&quot;para&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;p&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;style&quot;:&quot;margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;,&quot;data-layout-id&quot;:null},&quot;node&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;font&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;face&quot;:&quot;京華老宋体&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;}]"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">对于美国经历过的衰退和结构性变化。经济学家事后开出了很多药方，妄想避免被追赶经济体落后。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">这里至少在两个层面混淆了是否真的落后，</span></span><font face="京華老宋体"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;" data-pm-slice="1 1 [&quot;para&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;p&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;style&quot;:&quot;margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;,&quot;data-layout-id&quot;:null},&quot;node&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;font&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;face&quot;:&quot;京華老宋体&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;},&quot;node&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;font&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;face&quot;:&quot;京華老宋体&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;}]"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">一是忽略了美国本源优势的持续发展。二</span></span></font></font><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;" data-pm-slice="1 1 [&quot;para&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;p&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;style&quot;:&quot;margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;,&quot;data-layout-id&quot;:null},&quot;node&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;font&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;face&quot;:&quot;京華老宋体&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;}]"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">是夸大了从全面领先到部分领先，造成的结构性落后。</span></span></font></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;" data-pm-slice="1 1 [&quot;para&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;p&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;style&quot;:&quot;margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;,&quot;data-layout-id&quot;:null},&quot;node&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;font&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;face&quot;:&quot;京華老宋体&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;}]"><br  /></span></font></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;" data-pm-slice="1 1 [&quot;para&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;p&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;style&quot;:&quot;margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;,&quot;data-layout-id&quot;:null},&quot;node&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;font&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;face&quot;:&quot;京華老宋体&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;}]"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;text-decoration: underline;">美国自1776年建国以来，从传统工匠时期到工业电气化时期再到二战之后绵延至今的大科学时代，</span></span></font></font><font face="京華老宋体"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;" data-pm-slice="1 1 [&quot;para&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;p&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;style&quot;:&quot;margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;text-decoration: underline;">单从科学技术的发展角度来看，</span></span></font><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;text-decoration: underline;">几乎没有过任何中断。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">美国的比较优势虽然在原发性创新，但二战刚刚结束时，美国基本在各个领域都具备绝对优势。起初，后进国家从低端制造业做起，虽有比较优势但无绝对优势。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;text-decoration: underline;">&nbsp;但经过几十年的发展，以中国为例，我们在工程技术的研究实践、供应链、产业链这些领域，都取得了压倒性的优势。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">过去的美国，方方面面都比他国强，可以任挑一样自己最有优势的；现在的美国，有些领域，想做也做不了了。</span></span><font face="京華老宋体" style="text-align: left;color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;" data-pm-slice="1 1 [&quot;para&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;p&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;style&quot;:&quot;margin-top: 0px; margin-bottom: 0px;&quot;,&quot;data-pm-slice&quot;:&quot;3 3 []&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;,&quot;data-layout-id&quot;:null},&quot;node&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;font&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;face&quot;:&quot;京華老宋体&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;}]"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">让低附加值的制造业回流本土在市场交换逻辑上经不起推敲。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><br  /></span></p><h3 style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;" data-pm-slice="2 3 []"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">另外，通常认为金融自由化会导致不可逆的伤害。</span></span><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">笔者认为，金融自由化恰恰是重塑比较优势的一面镜子。个体最开始用脚投票，后来用自己的货币选票在消费、投资领域投票。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">它一定会促使不同国家对于某项资产的优劣进行全球化归一的思考，到底什么资产在全球的视角下是一个好的资产。</span></span></font></h3><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><br  /></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">重塑比较优势对于短期经济来讲会带来阵痛，对长期生产力发展又是非常宝贵的指引。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">不过残酷的是，比较优势关注的是市场也即他者需要什么，与各国资源禀赋擅长做什么还隔着一层。三十年河东三十年河西，一个大时代有一个大时代的需要，世界一定不会在每个时代都需要某个国家最擅长的东西，但一个勤劳奋斗的国家总能迎来自己的时刻。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><br  /></span></p><section style="text-align: left;margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">3.被追赶与现代化的迷思</span></span></section></font></b></h3><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;" data-pm-slice="3 3 []"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;" data-pm-slice="3 3 []"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">在先进国家进入被追赶时期后，我们可以造个新词，称此新时期为后现代、后工业化。然而造新词并不意味着理解了旧词。到底什么是现代化？不能粗劣的认为GDP增长了就是现代化。现代化的路径案例不等于现代化本身。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;" data-pm-slice="3 3 []"><font face="京華老宋体"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;" data-pm-slice="1 1 [&quot;para&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;p&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;style&quot;:&quot;margin-top: 0px; margin-bottom: 0px;&quot;,&quot;data-pm-slice&quot;:&quot;3 3 []&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;,&quot;data-layout-id&quot;:null},&quot;node&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;font&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;face&quot;:&quot;京華老宋体&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;}]"><br  /></span></font></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;" data-pm-slice="3 3 []"><font face="京華老宋体"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;" data-pm-slice="1 1 [&quot;para&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;p&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;style&quot;:&quot;margin-top: 0px; margin-bottom: 0px;&quot;,&quot;data-pm-slice&quot;:&quot;3 3 []&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;,&quot;data-layout-id&quot;:null},&quot;node&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;font&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;face&quot;:&quot;京華老宋体&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;}]"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">实际上，没有任何两个国家遵循完全相同的现代化模式。</span></span><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;" data-pm-slice="1 1 [&quot;para&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;p&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;style&quot;:&quot;margin-top: 0px; margin-bottom: 0px;&quot;,&quot;data-pm-slice&quot;:&quot;3 3 []&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;,&quot;data-layout-id&quot;:null},&quot;node&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;font&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;face&quot;:&quot;京華老宋体&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;}]"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">现代化更像是一个未完成状态，只是对当下人类进程的一个阶段性描述。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">它虽然包括</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">诸如工业、科学、民主、个人权利等明显特征，但这些都不是必要标准，更不能作为尺子去衡量指导其他国家。</span></span></font></font></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;" data-pm-slice="3 3 []"><font face="京華老宋体"><font face="京華老宋体"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;" data-pm-slice="1 1 [&quot;para&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;p&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;style&quot;:&quot;margin-top: 0px; margin-bottom: 0px;&quot;,&quot;data-pm-slice&quot;:&quot;3 3 []&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;,&quot;data-layout-id&quot;:null},&quot;node&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;font&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;face&quot;:&quot;京華老宋体&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;}]"><br  /></span></font></font></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;" data-pm-slice="3 3 []"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">承认各</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">国仍然处于同一条河流（现代化）中的不同位置。才可以开始解释</span></span><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">的上升周期大多开始自</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">冒险家对未来的梦想。他们中的一些佼佼者发现了领先于时代的机遇，创造出巨大财富。随后，投机者登场，他们怀揣对未来的幻想，以最大马力投入新事业。社会经济资源开始半裹挟半自愿的逐渐向新领域倾倒，市场上渐渐生产出满是噱头的垃圾。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">大众从怀疑到接受，从小心参与到押上身家性命还不够，把能借的钱都借到赌个未来。泡沫在人们最满怀期待的时候破灭了，留下一地鸡毛。这是群氓既无金钱又无幻想的悲惨时刻。他们</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">渴求能人、善人，渴求</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">政府买下自己手中的垃圾资产。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体" data-pm-slice="2 3 [&quot;para&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;p&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;style&quot;:&quot;margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;,&quot;data-layout-id&quot;:null}]"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">在危机时刻，资产负债表千疮百孔之时，企业内部是否真就无药可救？究竟是做一个随波逐流、在泡沫中狂热加杠杆的投机企业，还是成为像伯克希尔那样现金充沛、固守本业的少数？是坐以待毙地等死，还是果断认错、断臂求生？</span></span><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;" data-pm-slice="1 1 [&quot;para&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;p&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;style&quot;:&quot;margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">虽然留给个体的选择很少，但总还有个选择。</span></span></font></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体" data-pm-slice="2 3 textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">政府积极介入宏观经济管理，确实起到了决定性的作用。然而</span></span></font></font></font></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">政府的手段始终来源于居民收入，透支未来的程度也有极限。当利息支出超过财政收入的某个阈值，政府要么违约，要么印钞。如果社会也没有更多储蓄供政府借入，财政刺激就</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, 16px;">纯粹的通胀。年轻一代发现自己还不清学贷、买不起房、看不到养老金，而政府还在用他们的税收救楼市</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">/股市、落后产能，垃圾资产就无法顺利出清，新的动能也难以破壳而出。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">宏观经济研究中的总需求、总供给和货币问题都会导向一个源头，就是信心。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">信心比金坚。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">信心是什么呢？简单来说，从经济角度，就是人们对自身的资产负债表和对未来现金流折现有稳定</span></span><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, [&quot;para&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;p&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;style&quot;:&quot;margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">预期</span></span></font><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system,
