16px;">在和平年代，信心让</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">保守</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">的企业固守本业，让善于冒险的人开拓未来。在危机年代，信心让众人暂时搁置亏损、搁置争执，同仇敌忾，拿一些子孙后代的税收，去解决当下的问题。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;" data-pm-slice="3 3 []"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">而信心</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">如何得来</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">，并不是宏观经济理论的研究范畴。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><br  /></span></font></p><h3 style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;" data-pm-slice="2 3 []"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">4.&nbsp;</span></span><b><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">大国与岛国的信心来源</span></span></font></b></h3><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">日本经济90年代初泡沫破裂，在1996年前后稍有复苏迹象。但到1997年，桥本内阁在IMF优先平衡预算的主流观念影响下，采取了削减赤字的方案，结果引发了二次通缩螺旋。</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">IMF 给开的财政整顿的药方，日本用不了。日本人也理解不了为什么美国执行去杠杆路径可以有效。《</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">大衰退</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">》</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">一书，从抨击IMF</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">不懂日本为始，到借日本经验指责伯南克肤浅，并给全世界支招为终。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">货币政策和财政政策适用于什么样的国家，因地制宜。在这一点上，想把美国经验用于日本的IMF&nbsp;</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">和想把日本经验用于美国的人都忽视了：岛国经济体、大国经济体各自沉淀的实体经济逻辑与政策思维之间的差距比人们想象中的大得多。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">日本的经济主干是几大商社，上下游无数中小企业依附其生存。这个共生结构在繁荣阶段具备极强的协同优势，但危机来临时，便会形成刚性约束：核心主干巨头大而不能倒。否则大量中小企业会失去订单、资金与渠道，整个产业网络就会发生连锁断裂。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">同时，也难有小企业能以创新之力开辟一片新天地，成长为能够替代旧主干的巨型企业。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(0, 0, 0);">因而</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">日本即使遭遇泡沫破裂，也很难找到</span></span><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;" data-pm-slice="1 1 [&quot;para&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;p&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;,&quot;data-layout-id&quot;:null},&quot;node&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;font&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;face&quot;:&quot;京華老宋体&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;}]"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">另起</span></span></font><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-style: italic;">（注：笔者将在另一篇专门文章论述</span></span><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-style: italic;">日本的观念系统与文化特性</span></span></font><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">反观美国，自开国以来，从运河、铁路、石油、钢铁、电力，到当下的信息科技，一波波浪潮的底色就是毁灭与新生。</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;" data-pm-slice="1 1 [&quot;para&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;p&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;style&quot;:&quot;margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;,&quot;data-layout-id&quot;:null},&quot;node&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;font&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;face&quot;:&quot;京華老宋体&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;}]"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">旧时代的产业巨兽在最辉煌的时候轰然倒塌，大批企业破产清算。但资本、土地、劳动力这些生产要素，会从消亡的旧商业中释放出来。新力量踏着前人的养分，又再搭建起全新的产业帝国。大国，</span></span><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, [&quot;para&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;p&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;style&quot;:&quot;margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;,&quot;data-layout-id&quot;:null},&quot;node&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;font&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;face&quot;:&quot;京華老宋体&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;}]"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">有实力让任何企业推倒重来，</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;" data-pm-slice="1 1 [&quot;para&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;p&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;style&quot;:&quot;margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">旧的死，自有新的活。这种持续迭代的机制，正是它总能完成优势赛道切换的原因。</span></span></font></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><font &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;" data-pm-slice="1 1  /></span></font></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;" data-pm-slice="1 1 [&quot;para&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;p&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;style&quot;:&quot;margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">而且美国也可以通过美元霸权，将多出的流动性让全世界共同承担后果。</span></span></font></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">以</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">邻为壑</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">虽然常在道义上遭到弱国谴责，但不可否认，这确实是强国特有的招数——总</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">有更大的外部缓冲空间去消化国内的问题。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;" data-pm-slice="3 3 []"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">日美金融路径</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">的根本区别也就在此出现：金融工具作用的实体经济对象不同，能用的工具和预期结果也不一样。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;" data-pm-slice="3 3 []"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">用市场还是用行政力量，大国的选择有异曲同工之妙</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">。这时候中国如果作为一个大国，非要从日本的</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">学习什么经验，那危险就大了。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><h2 style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><b><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;font-weight: bold;">五、生产力、贸易范围、货币总量</span></span></font></b></h2><h2 style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><b><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><br  /></span></font></b></h2><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">如果</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">一个国家在某一时期的</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">生产力剧增，或者该国法币能</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">覆盖的贸易范围剧增，那</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">么</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">货币量增加就大概率不会导致通胀。困难在于生产力、贸易范围、货币总量</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">三者</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">时时变幻如混沌摆。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">笔者认为这也是宏观经济预测困难的根本原因。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);font-weight: bold;text-decoration: underline;">生产力提升</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;text-decoration: underline;">的最主要表现就是现有产品/服务越来越便宜；新增的，则功能越来越多、种类越来越丰富、性能越来越强大。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">但随之而来影响经济进一步增长的问题是：新产品/服务占用了消费者越来越多可支配收入和心智空间。</span></span><font face="SimSun"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;" data-pm-slice="1 1 [&quot;para&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;p&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;style&quot;:&quot;margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;,&quot;data-layout-id&quot;:null},&quot;node&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;font&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;face&quot;:&quot;SimSun&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;}]"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">当大量新信息涌入消费者的头脑，一部分会转化为经验、技术与认知，成为促进下一代生产创新的智识原料；但也有相当部分冗余、低质信息会分散人的注意力、造成认知过载，徒耗精力。</span></span></font></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">新消费是有益于人的智识提升，进一步提升生产力；还是抑制、甚至消灭人的智识，从而损毁未来的生产力？这不是一个必然有肯定答案的问题。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">更为复杂的是，</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">受限于国家治理结构与地理环境，</span></span><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;" data-pm-slice="1 1 [&quot;para&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;p&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;style&quot;:&quot;margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;,&quot;data-layout-id&quot;:null},&quot;node&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;font&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;face&quot;:&quot;京華老宋体&quot;},&quot;namespaceURI&quot;:&quot;http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml&quot;}]"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">大众智识与社会生产力的进步也有相当可能带来社会动荡，未必对经济体的增长有利。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">很多历史时期，人们会自动选择集体降智。</span></span></font><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">这是大历史观文章将要阐述的内容了。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);font-weight: bold;">货币量增加</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">的有利或不利，则主要看货币去向。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">如果一个经济体大部分的钱都是在境外作为贸易结算、支付手段、财富贮藏手段，那它对于本国未来的增长，既没有起到足够的货币乘数效应，也不会带来明显通胀。反之如果留在国内投入生产，拉动经济，就是另外一个故事。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><br  /></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">除了民间消费与投资，还有</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;text-decoration: underline;">基础设施建设等政府支出</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">。后者</span></span></font><font face="SimSun"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">能够显著降低交易成本、打通生产要素流动，放大生产力上限；但行政管控这类</span></span></font><font face="SimSun"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">“管理工具”，过少则令社会秩序缺失、交易成本飙升，过多则压制市场试错空间，增加企业制度性成本，反而抵消基建带来的红利。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;" data-pm-slice="3 3 []"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">总之，流动性停留在国内还是扩散到国际。</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">掌握了财富的人是做有利于提升一国生产力，还是用于做无益于生产力提升的事儿，是巨大的分野</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">。简单比较两个经济体的</span></span></font><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">M2水平就可能产生重大的误导。</span></span></font></p><p 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">如果说七十年代的美国完成了一次相对平滑的优势赛道切换，那么当下 AI 浪潮带来的新一轮重塑，正在暴露出更深层的结构性矛盾。</span></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">在可见的未来，美国仍将继续在人类科技前沿不断飞速狂奔。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;text-decoration: underline;">跨时代的产品，总要花很多年才能完全得到人们的接纳，</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;text-decoration: none;">无论在国内市场还是国际市场，均如此</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;text-decoration: underline;">结合国际博弈的因素，</span></span><span leaf="" data-pm-slice="1 1 [&quot;para&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;p&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;style&quot;:&quot;margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: underline;">落后国家暂时不需要也买不起</span></span><span leaf=""><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;text-decoration: underline;">价值极高的前沿科技成果；主要竞争国家想买，美国也不会卖。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">这就造成了一个尴尬现实：美国国计民生所需的农产品、工业品和消费品，必须从其他国家真金白银进口，但本国产出的高科技产品却难以等量出口。</span></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf="" data-pm-slice="1 1 YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">在他希望选择的成长道路上，总有百倍千倍于当下的协作机会。</span></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;"><span leaf=""><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">这二三十年，我们过惯了安全稳定的生活，或许会认为金钱、消费的增长空间已经有限。但看看今天的非洲、拉美、中东吧，再读读</span></span><span leaf=""><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">上世纪的伤痛文字，这些地方的多数民众仍和几十年前一样，饱受贫穷战乱的煎熬。欧洲腹地也频频冒出不和谐插曲。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">全世界的大部分地区都还处在不太平的状态。</span></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;"><span leaf=""><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">作为乐观技术主义者，我认为技术进步总可以令人类的境遇向好的方向发展，但各个地区需要的技术能力高低有别。在中国，当下的技术水平已足够维持一个统一、安全、便捷、稳定的社会。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;text-decoration: underline;">但在其他国家，还需要更强大的技术，令国家治理、个体权利之间按照他们的地理特征和文化观念求得平衡。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">&nbsp;</span></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;"><span leaf=""><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;"><span leaf=""><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">所以，当半数以上的人类进入到安静祥和状态时，对应的金融资产总量将是我们无法想象的规模。</span></span><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;" data-pm-slice="1 1 [&quot;para&quot;,{&quot;tagName&quot;:&quot;p&quot;,&quot;attributes&quot;:{&quot;style&quot;:&quot;margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align:  /></span></font></p><p style="margin-top: 0px;margin-bottom: 0px;text-align: left;" data-pm-slice="6 3 []"><font face="京華老宋体"><font face="京華老宋体"><font face="京華老宋体"><font face="京華老宋体"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">一路走来，经济学对我来说似真亦幻，有时觉得很有用，很神圣。有时面对美好理想与骨感现实，又觉得经济学毫无用处。但至今我仍认为</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">机会成本和需求定律是属于元知识级别的概念</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">，值得每个人好好揣摩，至少不排斥以这个出发点进行思考。</span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">上半年，因为南京旅行，开始了对日本南京大屠杀和731部队文字的阅读。从军事、政治，到社会、文化，自然而然的读到了经济金融。因为前几年阅读李录的《文明、现代化、价值投资与中国》时，文中详细引用过辜朝明的理论。就借这个机会读完了辜氏的《大衰退》和《被追赶的经济体》。</span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">日、美二战后在经济金融领域多次你来我往。两国宏观经济学家基于相近理论框架，仅仅因为国家基础的差异，就推导出截然不同的研究结果。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">这为思考宏观经济的作用与局限，提供了非常有价值的素材。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);">尤其是国人自清末开始，就多有以日本为鉴思考中国问题的习惯，便使得相关研究更有意义。</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">未曾想，读两本书的时间和写书评的时间几乎等量齐观。看着积累出的几千字书评，就想汇总一篇小文，说说宏观经济理论与经济危机的关系。</span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">我的思考重心离开宏观经济领域许久，个人投资风格也很抵触把宏观研究与投资实践绑在一起，所以本无计划在这个侧路旁支停留。无奈文字牵着人走。讨论的重点很快跳入对宏观经济方法论的局限性批判上。在深水区遨游了数月，经历过想批判一切的狂妄，也在研读主流经济学进展后，叹服其应对经济周期的有效性。思考终于停顿在半山腰处。</span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">并不满意，也没什么信心说此文可作为我在宏观经济领域的基石性文字。 就只是把当下能想到的都写出来了，冠以Draft之名，停在这里。</span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">现在是时候回到对日本主题了，继续开始 《岛国沉浮：困兽的悲剧宿命》的研究。</span></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><br  /></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><a class="normal_text_link mp_article_text_link" target="_blank" style="" href="https://mp.weixin.qq.com/s?__biz=MzI3OTA2MDA3NA==&amp;mid=2247487940&amp;idx=1&amp;sn=6733d439ce1a3a095dd6f14dedd227f9&amp;scene=21#wechat_redirect" textvalue="宏观经济中没有摆脱经济危机的圣杯（Draft）" data-itemshowtype="0" linktype="text" data-linktype="2"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">宏观经济中没有摆脱经济危机的圣杯（Draft）</span></a></span></p><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><br  /></span></p><section style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;" data-pm-slice="2 3 []"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;">Ps. 有朋友留言对最后关于未来的部分提出疑问，在这里补充说明下我对未来的思考思路：</span></span></section><section style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;" data-pm-slice="2 3 []"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><br  /></span></section><section style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span data-pm-slice="0 0 []"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;, &quot;Hiragino Sans GB&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei UI&quot;, &quot;Microsoft YaHei&quot;, Arial, sans-serif;line-height: 1.6;letter-spacing: 0.034em;font-style: normal;font-weight: normal;"><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;text-decoration: underline;">最后一部分确实逻辑跳跃。实际上，只是选取了各种可能未来的乐观分支加以描述，并不算逻辑论证。也鉴于本文主题，这些描述的依据和对其他情形的讨论将在另一篇有关大历史观的文章中再展开。当然，我想即便在那篇文章，</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);text-decoration: underline;">人们对未来的畅想也更多是在实践意义上做些梳理，为当下的</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);font-weight: bold;text-decoration: underline;">行动与信念</span><span textstyle="" style="font-size: 16px;color: rgb(64, 118, 0);text-decoration: underline;">提供些佐证，但绝对不会有水晶球般的必然性。</span></span></span></section><p style="margin-bottom: 0px;margin-top: 0px;"><span leaf="" style="color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.9);font-size: 17px;PingFang SC&quot;, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, &quot;Helvetica Neue&quot;,
